document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {

    const searchInputs = document.querySelectorAll('.search-input');
    const filterTagsDiv = document.querySelector('#filterTags');
    const columnCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.column-checkbox');
    const saveSearchForm = document.getElementById('saveSearchForm');
    const clearFiltersButton = document.getElementById('clearFiltersButton');
    const tableHeaders = document.querySelectorAll('th.resizable');
    const selectAllCheckbox =  document.getElementById('selectAllColumns');
    const tableContainer = document.querySelector('.table-container');
    const jsonScriptElement = document.getElementById('json-data');
    const jsonData = JSON.parse(jsonScriptElement.textContent);
    
    
    /* --  Search for input and listbox elements in serversTable, and save them in an object -- */
    const listboxes = document.querySelectorAll('#serversTable [id$="-listbox"]');
    const elements = {};
    
    listboxes.forEach(listbox => {
        const baseName = listbox.id.replace('-listbox', '');
        const input = document.querySelector(`#serversTable input[name="${baseName}"]`);
        elements[`${baseName}Listbox`] = listbox;
        elements[`${baseName}Input`] = input;
    });

    let filters = {};
    let selectedColumns = [];
    

    /* --  Functions -- */
    
    function clearFilters() {
    
        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
        const params = new URLSearchParams(url.search);
        
        serversTable=document.getElementById('serversTable');
        inputs = serversTable.getElementsByTagName('input');

        for (index = 0; index < inputs.length; ++index) {
            params.delete(inputs[index].name);

        }
    
        params.delete('page');
        if (tableContainer) {
            const currentScrollLeft = tableContainer.scrollLeft;
            params.set('scrollLeft', currentScrollLeft);  // Restore the scroll position
        }

        url.search = params.toString();
        window.location.href = url.toString();
        sessionStorage.removeItem('selectedSearchName');
        
    }


    function updateColumnVisibility() {
    
        const columnsToShow = Array.from(columnCheckboxes).filter(checkbox => checkbox.checked).map(checkbox => checkbox.getAttribute('data-column'));
        selectedColumns = columnsToShow;
        const allColumns = Array.from(columnCheckboxes).map(checkbox => checkbox.getAttribute('data-column'));
        allColumns.forEach(column => {
            const columnElements = document.querySelectorAll(`.${column}`);
            columnElements.forEach(element => {
                element.style.display = columnsToShow.includes(column) ? '' : 'none';
            });
        });

        // Update the URL with the current state of visible columns
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        urlParams.set('visible_columns', selectedColumns.join(','));
        history.replaceState(null, '', `${window.location.pathname}?${urlParams.toString()}`);
        
    }


    function createFilterTag(column, term) {

        const normalizedTerm = term.toUpperCase();


        if (filterTagsDiv.querySelector(`[data-column="${column}"][data-term="${normalizedTerm}"]`)) {  // Check if a filter tag is alread existing
            return;
        }
        if (filtersContainer.contains(noFiltersMessage)) {  // Remove the "No filter" text before adding a new filter button
            filtersContainer.removeChild(noFiltersMessage);
        } 

        const tag = document.createElement('span');
        tag.classList.add('filter-tag');
        tag.textContent = `${column}: ${normalizedTerm} `;
        tag.dataset.column = column;
        tag.dataset.term = normalizedTerm;

        const removeButton = document.createElement('span');
        removeButton.classList.add('remove-tag');
        removeButton.textContent = 'x';
        removeButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
        
            filters[column] = filters[column].filter(t => t !== normalizedTerm);
            if (filters[column].length === 0) {
                delete filters[column];
            }
            
            const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);  // Actualize URLSearchParams to reflect the deletion

            if (filters[column] && filters[column].length > 0) {
                urlParams.set(column, filters[column].join(','));
            } else {
                urlParams.delete(column);
            }

            filterTagsDiv.removeChild(tag);  // Remove the filter Tag
            history.replaceState(null, '', `${window.location.pathname}?${urlParams.toString()}`);  // Actualize the URL without reloading the page
            applyFilters();
        });

        tag.appendChild(removeButton);
        filterTagsDiv.appendChild(tag);

    }


    function logFilters() {

        console.log("Current state of filters", JSON.stringify(filters,null,2));

    }


    function handleInput(e) {
    
        if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target.value.trim() !== '') {
        
            e.preventDefault();
            const column = e.target.name;
            let terms;

            if (column === 'server') {
                terms = e.target.value.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[\'"]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ',').replace(/;/g, ',').split(',').map(term => term.trim());
            } else {
                terms = e.target.value.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[\'"]/g, '').split(',').map(term => term.trim());
            }
            const filtersContainer = document.getElementById('filters-container');

            if (filtersContainer.contains(noFiltersMessage)) {
                filtersContainer.removeChild(noFiltersMessage);
            }

            if (!filters[column]) {
                filters[column] = [];
            }

            terms.forEach(term => {
                if (!filters[column].includes(term)) {
                    filters[column].push(term);
                    createFilterTag(column, term);
                }
            });

            e.target.value = '';  // Clear the search field after adding the terms
            applyFilters();
            logFilters()
            
        }
        
    }


    function restoreColumnVisibilityFromURL() {
    
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const visibleColumns = urlParams.get('visible_columns');
        
        if (visibleColumns) {
            const columns = visibleColumns.split(',');
            columnCheckboxes.forEach(checkbox => {
                checkbox.checked = columns.includes(checkbox.getAttribute('data-column'));
            });
            updateColumnVisibility();
        }
        
    }
    

    window.applyFilters = function () {
    
        // The applyFilters function is designed to take user-selected filters and other parameters, process them, and update the URL query parameters accordingly. 
        // It ensures that the filters are normalized (values converted to uppercase and duplicates removed), some specific keys are handled separately, 
        // and multiple aspects of the filter state are preserved when updating the URL.
    
        let debugMode = false;
        const searchParams = new URLSearchParams();
        
        const specialKeys = Object.values(jsonData.fields)  // Extract the listid entries from the json file: these represent the columns search dropdown boxes
        	.map(field => field.listid)
        	.filter(id => id !== undefined);

        for (const key in filters) {
            if (filters[key].length > 0) {
                const normalizedTerms = [...new Set(filters[key].map(value => value.toUpperCase()))].join(',');

                if (specialKeys.includes(key)) {
                    searchParams.set(key, normalizedTerms);
                } else {
                    searchParams.append(key, normalizedTerms);
                }
            }
        }

        if (selectedColumns.length > 0) {
            searchParams.append('visible_columns', selectedColumns.join(','));
        }

        const pageSizeSelect = document.getElementById('page_size');
        const selectedPageSize = pageSizeSelect ? pageSizeSelect.value : 50;
        searchParams.set('page_size', selectedPageSize);

        const currentParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        for (const [key, value] of currentParams.entries()) {
            if (key.startsWith('cat_')) {
                searchParams.set(key, value);
            }
        }

        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        for (const [key, value] of urlParams.entries()) {
            if (!filters[key] || filters[key].length === 0) {
                continue;
            }
            if (!searchParams.has(key)) {
                searchParams.set(key, value);
            }
        }

        if (tableContainer) {
            const currentScrollLeft = tableContainer.scrollLeft;
            searchParams.set('scrollLeft', currentScrollLeft);
        }

        if (!debugMode) {
            window.location.search = searchParams.toString();
        }

        logFilters();
        
    };


    function toggleSelectAll() {
    
        const selectAllCheckbox = document.getElementById('selectAllColumns');
        const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.column-checkbox');
        const isChecked = selectAllCheckbox.checked;  // Change the state of all checkboxes based on the state of 'Select All'
        checkboxes.forEach(checkbox => {
            checkbox.checked = isChecked;
        });

        updateColumnVisibility();  // Update the visibility of the columns and apply filters after changing the state
        applyFilters();
        
    }


    function updateSelectAllState() {
    
        const selectAllCheckbox = document.getElementById('selectAllColumns');
        const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.column-checkbox');
        const allChecked = Array.from(checkboxes).every(checkbox => checkbox.checked);
        const someChecked = Array.from(checkboxes).some(checkbox => checkbox.checked);

        if (allChecked) {  // Update the state of 'Select All' based on the conditions
            selectAllCheckbox.checked = true;
            selectAllCheckbox.indeterminate = false;
        } else if (someChecked) {
            selectAllCheckbox.checked = false;
            selectAllCheckbox.indeterminate = true;  // Display the script if some are marked
        } else {
            selectAllCheckbox.checked = false;
            selectAllCheckbox.indeterminate = false;
        }
        
    }
    

    function attachCategoryHandlers() {
    
        const categoryCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.category-checkbox');

        categoryCheckboxes.forEach(function(catCheckbox) {
            const categoryId = catCheckbox.getAttribute('data-category');
            const content = document.getElementById(categoryId);
            if (!content) return;
            const childCheckboxes = content.querySelectorAll('.column-checkbox');

            catCheckbox.addEventListener('change', function () {  // When category checkbox changes, update only non-disabled child checkboxes
                childCheckboxes.forEach(function (child) {
                    if (!child.disabled) {
                        child.checked = catCheckbox.checked;
                    }
                    child.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
                });

                catCheckbox.indeterminate = false;
                 //applyFilters();
            });

            childCheckboxes.forEach(function (child) {  // When child checkboxes change, just call the clean-up function
                child.addEventListener('change', function () {
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        updateCategoryStates();
                        //applyFilters();
                    }, 0);
                });
            });

        });
        
    }


    function updateCategoryStates() {
    
        const categoryCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.category-checkbox');

        categoryCheckboxes.forEach(catCheckbox => {
            const categoryId = catCheckbox.getAttribute('data-category');
            const content = document.getElementById(categoryId);

            if (!content) return;
            const childCheckboxes = content.querySelectorAll('.column-checkbox');
            const totalChildren = childCheckboxes.length;
            const checkedChildren = Array.from(childCheckboxes).filter(c => c.checked).length;

            if (checkedChildren === totalChildren) {
                catCheckbox.checked = true;
                catCheckbox.indeterminate = false;
            } else if (checkedChildren === 0) {
                catCheckbox.checked = false;
                catCheckbox.indeterminate = false;
            } else {
                catCheckbox.checked = false;
                catCheckbox.indeterminate = true;
            }
        });
        
    }


    function restoreCategoryStateFromURL() {
    
      document.querySelectorAll('.category-content').forEach(content => {
        const categoryId = content.id; // ej: "cat1"
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const state = urlParams.get('cat_' + categoryId);  // It is expected that a parameter will be provided, such as cat_cat1 = "true" (expanded) or false (collapsed)
        if (state === null || state === "false") {
          content.classList.add('hidden');
        } else if (state === "true") {
          content.classList.remove('hidden');
        }
      });
      
    }


    function updateCategoryStateInURL(categoryId, isExpanded) {
    
      const url = new URL(window.location.href);
      const urlParams = url.searchParams;
      urlParams.set('cat_' + categoryId, isExpanded ? "true" : "false");  // Set the parameter 'cat_' to 'true' or 'false'
      history.replaceState(null, '', url.pathname + '?' + urlParams.toString());  // Update the URL without reloading the page
      
    }


    function attachCategoryToggleHandlers() {
    
      const categoryTitles = document.querySelectorAll('.category-title');
      categoryTitles.forEach(title => {
        title.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
          e.stopPropagation();
          const targetId = this.getAttribute('data-target');
          const content = document.getElementById(targetId);
          if (content) {
            content.classList.toggle('hidden');
            const isExpanded = !content.classList.contains('hidden');
            updateCategoryStateInURL(targetId, isExpanded);  // Update the URL with the current state of this category
          }
        });
      });
      
    }
    

    function applyPageSize() {

        const pageSizeSelect = document.getElementById('page_size');
        const selectedPageSize = pageSizeSelect.value;

        const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        searchParams.set('page_size', selectedPageSize);
        searchParams.delete('page');  // Reset the page when resizing

        window.location.search = searchParams.toString();
        
    }


    function hasActiveFilters() {
    
        const filterTags = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-tag');
        if (filterTagsDiv.length === 0) {
            filterTagsDiv.textContent = 'No filters';
            sessionStorage.removeItem('selectedSearchName');
        } else {
            //filterTagsDiv.textContent = ''; // Clear previous text if any
        }
        
        return filterTags.length > 0;
        
    }


    function validateAndShowSaveSearchModal() {
    
        if (hasActiveFilters()) {
            showSaveSearchModal()
        } else {
            alert("Please apply at least one filter before saving the search.");
        }
        
    }


    function initColumnResize() {
    
        const tableHeaders = document.querySelectorAll('th.resizable');
        const tableContainer = document.querySelector('.table-container');

        tableHeaders.forEach(header => {
        
            const resizer = document.createElement('div');
            resizer.classList.add('resizer');
            resizer.style.width = '5px';
            resizer.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
            resizer.style.position = 'absolute';
            resizer.style.right = '0';
            resizer.style.top = '0';
            resizer.style.bottom = '0';
            resizer.style.zIndex = '1';
            header.appendChild(resizer);

            resizer.addEventListener('mousedown', initResize);

            function initResize(e) {
            
                e.stopPropagation();
                e.preventDefault();
                const startX = e.clientX;
                const startWidth = header.offsetWidth;
                const initialScrollLeft = tableContainer.scrollLeft;

                document.body.style.cursor = 'col-resize';
                document.body.style.userSelect = 'none';
                window.addEventListener('mousemove', resizeColumn);  // Calculate the initial scroll position and adjust the column width on mousemove
                window.addEventListener('mouseup', stopResize);

                function resizeColumn(e) {
                    const scrollDiff = tableContainer.scrollLeft - initialScrollLeft;
                    const deltaX = e.clientX - startX + scrollDiff;
                    const newWidth = startWidth + deltaX;

                    if (newWidth > 50) {
                        header.style.width = `${newWidth}px`;
                    }
                }

                function stopResize() {
                    window.removeEventListener('mousemove', resizeColumn);
                    window.removeEventListener('mouseup', stopResize);
                    document.body.style.cursor = '';
                    document.body.style.userSelect = '';
                }
                
            }
            
        });
        
    }

    
    /* --  Elements Events and Actions -- */

    columnCheckboxes.forEach(checkbox => {
        checkbox.addEventListener('change', function () {
            //updateSelectAllState(); 
            updateColumnVisibility();
            //applyFilters();
        });
    });


    searchInputs.forEach(input => {  // Actions on input fields 
        input.addEventListener('keyup', handleInput);
    });


    if (selectAllCheckbox) {
       selectAllCheckbox.addEventListener('change', toggleSelectAll);
    }


    if (clearFiltersButton) {
        clearFiltersButton.addEventListener('click', clearFilters);
    }
    
    
    Object.keys(elements).forEach(key => {  // For each listbox filter and input filter
        if (key.endsWith('Listbox')) {
            const listbox = elements[key];
            const baseName = key.replace('Listbox', '');
            const input = elements[`${baseName}Input`];

            if (listbox && input) {
                listbox.addEventListener('change', function() {
                    const selectedValue = listbox.value.toUpperCase();

                    if (selectedValue) {
                        input.value = '';
                        const selectedValueStrict = '@' + selectedValue;

                        if (!filters[baseName]) {
                            filters[baseName] = [];
                        }

                        if (!filters[baseName].includes(selectedValueStrict)) {
                            filters[baseName].push(selectedValueStrict);
                        }

                        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
                        urlParams.delete(baseName);

                        filters[baseName].forEach(value => {
                            createFilterTag(baseName, value);
                        });

                        urlParams.set(baseName, filters[baseName].join(','));
                        history.replaceState(null, '', `${window.location.pathname}?${urlParams.toString()}`);
                        applyFilters();
                    }
                });

                const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);  // Handle URL parameters for this listbox
                const paramValue = urlParams.get(baseName);

                if (paramValue) {
                    listbox.value = paramValue;
                    input.value = paramValue;
                    input.value = ''; // Clear the input value
                }
            }
        }
        
    });


    /* --  Rebuild the interface -- */

    restoreColumnVisibilityFromURL();
    updateColumnVisibility();
    attachCategoryHandlers();  // Attach handlers to the category checkboxes
    updateCategoryStates();  // Recalculate category states (to show 'indeterminate' if necessary)
    restoreCategoryStateFromURL()
    attachCategoryToggleHandlers()


	if (saveSearchForm) {
	
		saveSearchForm.addEventListener('submit', function(event) {
        	event.preventDefault();
        	const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        	const filters = Object.fromEntries(searchParams.entries());

        	if (Object.keys(filters).length === 0) {
            	alert("No filters are selected. Please select at least one filter.");
            	return;
        	}
        	
        	const inputName = document.getElementById('search_name');
            const chosenName = inputName.value.trim();
            if (chosenName.length > 25) {
                alert("Search name must be less than 25 characters.");
                return;
            }
            if (savedSearchNames.includes(chosenName)) {
                event.preventDefault();
                alert("A search with this name already exists.");
                return;
            }
        	
        	document.getElementById('filtersInput').value = JSON.stringify(filters);

        	const tags = [];
        	filterTagsDiv.querySelectorAll('.filter-tag').forEach(tag => {
            	tags.push(tag.dataset.column + ':' + tag.dataset.term);
        	});
        	document.getElementById('tagsInput').value = JSON.stringify(tags);
        	saveSearchForm.submit();
    	});
    	
	}


    const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    for (const [key, value] of urlParams.entries()) {
        if (key !== 'page' && key !== 'visible_columns' && key !== 'page_size' && key !== 'scrollLeft' && (!key.startsWith('cat_')) ) {
            if (!filters[key]) {
                filters[key] = [];
            }
            const terms = value.split(',').map(term => term.trim().toUpperCase());
            terms.forEach(term => {
                if (!filters[key].includes(term)) {
                    filters[key].push(term);
                    createFilterTag(key, term);
                }
            });
        }
    }

    updateColumnVisibility();

    window.toggleSelectAll = toggleSelectAll;
    window.updateSelectAllState = updateSelectAllState;
    window.applyPageSize = applyPageSize;  // Make it globally accessible
    window.validateAndShowSaveSearchModal = validateAndShowSaveSearchModal;  // Make it globally accessible

    initColumnResize();
    updateSelectAllState();


    /* --  Restore the scroll position if it comes in the URL -- */

    {
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const storedScrollLeft = urlParams.get('scrollLeft');
        if (storedScrollLeft && tableContainer) {
            const scrollLeftValue = parseInt(storedScrollLeft, 10);
            if (!isNaN(scrollLeftValue)) {
                tableContainer.scrollLeft = scrollLeftValue;
            }
            urlParams.delete('scrollLeft');  // Remove scrollLeft from the URL to avoid perpetuating it
            history.replaceState(null, '', window.location.pathname + '?' + urlParams.toString());
        }
    }

});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {  // Wait for the DOM to be fully loaded before executing the script

    // Get needed references
    const editModalOverlay = document.getElementById('editModalOverlay');
    const closeModalButton = document.getElementById('closeModalButton');
    const cancelModalButton = document.getElementById('cancelModalButton');
    const editForm = document.getElementById('editForm');
    var serversTable = document.getElementById('serversTable');
    var userCanEdit = serversTable.dataset.userCanedit === 'True';

    if (userCanEdit) {

        // Close button inside bulk edit modal
        const bulkModalClose = document.getElementById('bulkEditModal')
                                       .querySelector('.close-button');
        if (bulkModalClose) {
            bulkModalClose.addEventListener('click', closeBulkModal);
        }

        // Standalone bulk edit button — may no longer exist if grouped under Edit dropdown
        const bulkEditBtn = document.getElementById('bulkEditButton');
        if (bulkEditBtn) {
            bulkEditBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                document.getElementById('bulkEditModal').style.display = 'flex';
            });
        }

    }

    
    function openModal() {  // Open the edit modal and focus on the first input field in the form
        editModalOverlay.style.display = 'flex';
        const firstInput = document.getElementById('id_in_live_play');
        if (firstInput) firstInput.focus();
    }

    
    function closeModal() {  // Close the edit modal
        editModalOverlay.style.display = 'none';
    }

    
    document.querySelectorAll('.edit-button').forEach(button => {  // Add an event listener to each edit button

        button.addEventListener('click', function () {

            currentServerId = this.dataset.serverId;
            const editForm = document.getElementById('editForm');
            const historyContainer = document.getElementById('historyContainer');
            if (historyContainer.style.display === 'block') {
                historyContainer.style.display = 'none';
                editForm.style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('historyButton').style.display = 'inline-block';
            }

            const hostnameUnique = this.dataset.serverId;  // Get the hostname unique ID from the button's dataset
            const row = this.closest('tr');  // Get the row that the button is in
            const priorityAsset = row.dataset.priorityAsset;  // Get the values for the row from the dataset
            const inLivePlay = row.dataset.inLivePlay;
            const actionDuringLp = row.dataset.actionDuringLp;
            const actionDuringLpHistory = row.dataset.actionDuringLpHistory;
            const actionDuringLpHistoryLines = actionDuringLpHistory.split('\n').filter(line => !line.includes('EMPTY'));
            const originalActionDuringLp = row.dataset.originalActionDuringLp;
            const originalActionDuringLpHistory = row.dataset.originalActionDuringLpHistory;
            const originalActionDuringLpHistoryLines = originalActionDuringLpHistory.split('\n').filter(line => !line.includes('EMPTY'));
            const cluster = row.dataset.cluster;
            const clusterType = row.dataset.clusterType;

            // Inject the values into the edit form
            document.getElementById('editModalLabel').innerText = `Edit the information for ${hostnameUnique}`;
            document.getElementById('id_priority_asset').value = priorityAsset === 'EMPTY' ? '' : priorityAsset;
            document.getElementById('id_in_live_play').value = inLivePlay === 'EMPTY' ? '' : inLivePlay;
            document.getElementById('id_action_during_lp').value = actionDuringLp === 'EMPTY' ? '' : actionDuringLp;
            document.getElementById('id_original_action_during_lp_history').value = originalActionDuringLpHistoryLines.join('\n');
            document.getElementById('id_original_action_during_lp').value = originalActionDuringLp === 'EMPTY' ? '' : originalActionDuringLp;
            document.getElementById('id_action_during_lp_history').value = actionDuringLpHistoryLines.join('\n');
            document.getElementById('id_cluster').value = cluster === 'EMPTY' ? '' : cluster;
            document.getElementById('id_cluster_type').value = clusterType === 'EMPTY' ? '' : clusterType;

            if (!userCanEdit) {
                // Set all input fields to readonly
                document.querySelectorAll('#editForm input, #editForm select, #editForm textarea').forEach(field => {
                    field.readOnly = true;
                    field.disabled = true; // Disable select and textarea fields
                });
                document.querySelector('#editForm button[type="submit"]').style.display = 'none';
                document.querySelector('#editForm .btn-secondary').innerText = 'Close';
            }
            else {
                editForm.action = `/${appname}/edit/${hostnameUnique}/`;  // Set the form action to the edit URL for the hostname unique ID
            }
            
            openModal();
            
        });

    });

    
    closeModalButton.addEventListener('click', function () {  // Event listener to the close button in the edit modal
        closeModal();
        closeBulkModal();
    });

    
    cancelModalButton.addEventListener('click', function () {  // Event listener to the cancel button in the edit modal
        closeModal();
        closeBulkModal();
    });

    
    editModalOverlay.addEventListener('click', function (event) {  // Event listener to the edit modal overlay: If the user clicks on the overlay, close the modal
        if (event.target === editModalOverlay) {
            closeModal();
        }
    });

    
    document.getElementById('bulkEditModal').addEventListener('click', function (event) {  // Event listener to the bulk edit modal: If the user clicks on the modal background, close the modal
        if (event.target === this) {
            closeBulkModal();
        }
    });

    
    document.getElementById('bulkEditModal').querySelector('.btn-secondary').addEventListener('click', closeBulkModal);  // Event listener to the secondary button in the bulk edit modal


    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {  // Event listener for the Escape key
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            closeModal();
            closeBulkModal();
        }
    });
    

    document.getElementById('historyButton').addEventListener('click', function() {
    
        const editForm = document.getElementById('editForm');
        const historyContainer = document.getElementById('historyContainer');
        const historyButton = document.getElementById('historyButton');

        // Display the historic
        historyContainer.style.display = 'block';
        editForm.style.display = 'none';
        historyButton.style.display = 'none';

        // Get the current server ID
        const serverId = currentServerId

        const fieldNames = {
            "priority_asset": "Priority Asset",
            "in_live_play": "In Live Play",
            "action_during_lp": "Action During LP",
            "original_action_during_lp": "Original Action During LP",
            "cluster": "Cluster",
            "cluster_type": "Cluster Type"
        };

        // Get the historic data from the server
        fetch(`${getServerHistoryUrl}?server_id=${serverId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                const historyList = document.getElementById('historyList');
                historyList.innerHTML = '';

                // Sort the historic entries by descending date
                data.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));

                data.forEach(entry => {
                    const listItem = document.createElement('li');
                    listItem.classList.add('history-entry');
                    listItem.textContent = `${entry.date} - ${entry.user} - ${entry.fromsource}`;
                    const changesList = document.createElement('ul');
                    changesList.classList.add('changes-list');
                    Object.keys(entry.changes).forEach(field => {
                        const changeItem = document.createElement('li');
                        changeItem.classList.add('change-item');
                        if (entry.changes[field].info) {
                                changeItem.textContent = `${fieldNames[field]}: ${entry.changes[field].info}`;
                            } else {
                                changeItem.textContent = `${fieldNames[field]}: ${entry.changes[field].new} (was ${entry.changes[field].old})`;
                            }
                        changesList.appendChild(changeItem);
                    });
                    listItem.appendChild(changesList);
                    historyList.appendChild(listItem);
                });
            });

    });


    document.getElementById('backToEditButton').addEventListener('click', function() {
        const editForm = document.getElementById('editForm');
        const historyContainer = document.getElementById('historyContainer');
        const historyButton = document.getElementById('historyButton');

        // Display the edit form
        historyContainer.style.display = 'none';
        editForm.style.display = 'block';
        historyButton.style.display = 'inline-block';
        
    }); 
    

});

<script>
(function () {
'use strict';

// ── Data from Django ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const SAVED_TEXTS   = JSON.parse('{{ saved_texts|escapejs }}');
const DEFAULT_TEXTS = JSON.parse('{{ default_texts|escapejs }}');
const ALL_RULES     = JSON.parse('{{ all_rules|escapejs }}');
const CSRF          = '{{ csrf_token }}';
const IS_EDITOR     = {{ is_user_editor|yesno:"true,false" }};

const URL_DC      = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_datacenters' %}";
const URL_TEXTS   = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_texts' %}";
const URL_PREVIEW = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_preview' %}";
const URL_START   = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_start' %}";
const URL_STATUS  = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_job_status' job_id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' %}";

// Color per rule key (matches SVG result box colors)
const RULE_COLORS = {
    cluster_yes_lp_yes: '#ce93d8',
    cluster_yes_lp_no:  '#f48fb1',
    no_yes_phys:        '#90caf9',
    no_yes_virt_fixed:  '#90caf9',
    no_yes_virt_str:    '#ffd54f',
    no_no_phys:         '#a5d6a7',
    no_no_virt_fixed:   '#a5d6a7',
    no_no_virt_str:     '#ffd54f',
    hypervisor:         '#b0e3e6',
};

const RULE_LABELS = {
    cluster_yes_lp_yes: 'A — Cluster YES · LP YES',
    cluster_yes_lp_no:  'B — Cluster YES · LP NO',
    no_yes_phys:        'C — Cluster NO · LP YES · Physical',
    no_yes_virt_fixed:  'D — Cluster NO · LP YES · Virtual Fixed',
    no_yes_virt_str:    'E — Cluster NO · LP YES · Virtual Stretched',
    no_no_phys:         'F — Cluster NO · LP NO · Physical',
    no_no_virt_fixed:   'G — Cluster NO · LP NO · Virtual Fixed',
    no_no_virt_str:     'H — Cluster NO · LP NO · Virtual Stretched',
    hypervisor:         'I — Machine Type HYPERVISOR',
};

// Letter (as used in the "Import Actions" CSV) -> rule key
const LETTER_TO_RULE = {
    A: 'cluster_yes_lp_yes',
    B: 'cluster_yes_lp_no',
    C: 'no_yes_phys',
    D: 'no_yes_virt_fixed',
    E: 'no_yes_virt_str',
    F: 'no_no_phys',
    G: 'no_no_virt_fixed',
    H: 'no_no_virt_str',
    I: 'hypervisor',
};

// ── State ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let currentStep  = 1;
let selectedDC   = null;
let pollInterval = null;

// ── Stepper ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function updateStepper() {
    for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
        const sc  = document.getElementById(`sc${i}`);
        const sl  = document.getElementById(`sl${i}`);
        const sep = document.getElementById(`sep${i}`);
        sc.className = 'step-circle';
        sl.className = 'step-label';
        if (sep) sep.className = 'step-sep';
        if (i < currentStep) {
            sc.classList.add('done'); sc.textContent = '✓';
            sl.classList.add('done');
            if (sep) sep.classList.add('done');
        } else if (i === currentStep) {
            sc.classList.add('active'); sc.textContent = i;
            sl.classList.add('active');
        } else {
            sc.textContent = i;
        }
    }
}

function goStep(n) {
    document.getElementById(`step${currentStep}`).classList.remove('visible');
    currentStep = n;
    document.getElementById(`step${currentStep}`).classList.add('visible');
    updateStepper();
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    if (n === 2) loadDiagramSvg();
}

// ── Step 1: datacenters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function loadDatacenters() {
    fetch(URL_DC)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(data => {
            const grid = document.getElementById('dcGrid');
            grid.innerHTML = '';
            if (!data.datacenters || !data.datacenters.length) {
                grid.innerHTML = '<p class="dc-loading">No datacenters found.</p>';
                return;
            }
            const lastDC = localStorage.getItem('bc_last_datacenter');
            data.datacenters.forEach(dc => {
                const card = document.createElement('div');
                card.className = 'dc-card';
                card.innerHTML = `<div class="dc-name">${escHtml(dc.datacenter)}</div>
                                  <div class="dc-count">${dc.count} <span>unique servers</span></div>
                                  <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:2px;">(${dc.count_total} total occurrences)</div>`;
                card.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    document.querySelectorAll('.dc-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected'));
                    card.classList.add('selected');
                    selectedDC = dc.datacenter;
                    localStorage.setItem('bc_last_datacenter', dc.datacenter);
                    document.getElementById('btn1Next').disabled = false;
                });
                grid.appendChild(card);

                // Pre-select last used DC
                if (dc.datacenter === lastDC) {
                    card.classList.add('selected');
                    selectedDC = dc.datacenter;
                    document.getElementById('btn1Next').disabled = false;
                }
            });
        })
        .catch(() => {
            document.getElementById('dcGrid').innerHTML =
                '<p class="dc-loading">Error loading datacenters.</p>';
        });
}

// ── Step 2: texts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Debounce helper
function debounce(fn, delay) {
    let timer;
    return (...args) => { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(() => fn(...args), delay); };
}

// Autosave rule texts to DB (debounced — fires 1.5s after last keystroke)
const autoSaveTexts = debounce(() => {
    fetch(URL_TEXTS, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': CSRF },
        body: JSON.stringify({ rule_texts: getRuleTexts() }),
    }).catch(() => {}); // silent — non-critical
}, 1500);

function initTexts() {
    // Load saved texts (from DB via Django template variable)
    let hasSaved = false;
    ALL_RULES.forEach(key => {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`inp_${key}`);
        if (!inp) return;
        inp.value = SAVED_TEXTS[key] !== undefined
            ? SAVED_TEXTS[key]
            : (DEFAULT_TEXTS[key] || '');
        if (SAVED_TEXTS[key] !== undefined && SAVED_TEXTS[key] !== DEFAULT_TEXTS[key]) hasSaved = true;
        // Autosave on every change
        inp.addEventListener('input', autoSaveTexts);
    });
    if (hasSaved) {
        document.getElementById('savedNotice').style.display = 'block';
    }
}

function restoreDefault(ruleKey) {
    const inp = document.getElementById(`inp_${ruleKey}`);
    if (!inp) return;
    inp.value = DEFAULT_TEXTS[ruleKey] || '';
    updateSvgCell(ruleKey, inp.value);
}

function getRuleTexts() {
    const out = {};
    ALL_RULES.forEach(key => {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`inp_${key}`);
        out[key]  = inp ? inp.value.trim() : (DEFAULT_TEXTS[key] || '');
    });
    return out;
}

// ── Import Actions (CSV: "Action;Text", one row per letter A-I) ────────────────
let pendingImportTexts = null;

// Parses and strictly validates the CSV. Returns { ok:true, texts } or { ok:false, errors }.
function parseActionsCsv(raw) {
    const lines = String(raw).split(/\r\n|\r|\n/).map(l => l.trim()).filter(l => l.length > 0);
    if (!lines.length) {
        return { ok: false, errors: ["The file is empty."] };
    }

    const letterRe = /^[A-Ia-i]$/;

    // Optional header row: skipped only if its first column isn't a valid letter.
    let dataLines  = lines;
    let lineOffset = 1;
    if (!letterRe.test(lines[0].split(';')[0].trim())) {
        dataLines  = lines.slice(1);
        lineOffset = 2;
    }
    if (!dataLines.length) {
        return { ok: false, errors: ["No data rows found after the header."] };
    }

    const errors = [];
    const seenAt = {};   // letter -> line number of first occurrence
    const texts  = {};   // rule_key -> text

    dataLines.forEach((line, idx) => {
        const lineNo  = idx + lineOffset;
        const sepIdx  = line.indexOf(';');
        if (sepIdx === -1) {
            errors.push(`Line ${lineNo}: missing ";" separator ("${line}").`);
            return;
        }

        const letterRaw = line.slice(0, sepIdx).trim();
        const letter     = letterRaw.toUpperCase();
        let text          = line.slice(sepIdx + 1).trim();
        if (text.length >= 2 && text.startsWith('"') && text.endsWith('"')) {
            text = text.slice(1, -1).trim();
        }

        if (!letterRe.test(letter)) {
            errors.push(`Line ${lineNo}: unknown action "${letterRaw}" (expected A to I).`);
            return;
        }
        if (seenAt[letter] !== undefined) {
            errors.push(`Line ${lineNo}: action "${letter}" is duplicated (already seen on line ${seenAt[letter]}).`);
            return;
        }
        seenAt[letter] = lineNo;

        if (!text) {
            errors.push(`Line ${lineNo} (action ${letter}): empty text.`);
            return;
        }

        texts[LETTER_TO_RULE[letter]] = text;
    });

    const missing = Object.keys(LETTER_TO_RULE).filter(l => seenAt[l] === undefined);
    if (missing.length) {
        errors.push(`Missing action(s) in the file: ${missing.join(', ')}.`);
    }

    if (errors.length) {
        return { ok: false, errors };
    }
    return { ok: true, texts };
}

function handleImportFile(event) {
    const file = event.target.files[0];
    event.target.value = ''; // allow re-selecting the same file next time
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
        const result = parseActionsCsv(reader.result);
        if (!result.ok) {
            pendingImportTexts = null;
            renderImportModal('error', result.errors);
        } else {
            pendingImportTexts = result.texts;
            renderImportModal('confirm', result.texts);
        }
    };
    reader.onerror = () => renderImportModal('error', ["Impossible de lire le fichier."]);
    reader.readAsText(file);
}

function renderImportModal(mode, payload) {
    const title  = document.getElementById('importModalTitle');
    const body   = document.getElementById('importModalBody');
    const footer = document.getElementById('importModalFooter');

    if (mode === 'error') {
        title.textContent = '✕ Invalid file';
        body.innerHTML =
            '<div class="alert alert-err" style="margin-top:0;">' +
            '<ul class="import-error-list">' +
            payload.map(e => `<li>${escHtml(e)}</li>`).join('') +
            '</ul></div>';
        footer.innerHTML = '<button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeImportModal()">Close</button>';
    } else {
        title.textContent = '⚠ Replace all 9 actions?';
        body.innerHTML =
            '<p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text-muted);margin-bottom:10px;">' +
            'This will overwrite the current text for all 9 actions below (and in the diagram). ' +
            'This set of texts is shared across all datacenters.</p>' +
            '<table class="confirm-table"><thead><tr><th>Action</th><th>Text</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
            Object.keys(LETTER_TO_RULE).map(letter => {
                const t = payload[LETTER_TO_RULE[letter]];
                return `<tr><td><strong>${letter}</strong></td><td>${escHtml(t)}</td></tr>`;
            }).join('') +
            '</tbody></table>';
        footer.innerHTML =
            '<button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeImportModal()">Cancel</button>' +
            '<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="confirmImport()">Replace all 9 actions</button>';
    }

    document.getElementById('importModalOverlay').classList.add('open');
}

function closeImportModal() {
    document.getElementById('importModalOverlay').classList.remove('open');
    pendingImportTexts = null;
}

function closeImportModalOverlay(event) {
    if (event.target === document.getElementById('importModalOverlay')) {
        closeImportModal();
    }
}

function confirmImport() {
    if (!pendingImportTexts) return;
    const texts = pendingImportTexts;

    ALL_RULES.forEach(key => {
        const text = texts[key];
        const inp  = document.getElementById(`inp_${key}`);
        if (text === undefined || !inp) return;
        inp.value = text;
        updateSvgCell(key, text);
    });

    fetch(URL_TEXTS, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': CSRF },
        body: JSON.stringify({ rule_texts: texts }),
    })
    .then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('save failed'); })
    .then(() => {
        document.getElementById('savedNotice').style.display = 'block';
    })
    .catch(() => {
        alert('Texts were updated on screen but saving to the server failed. Please retry.');
    })
    .finally(() => {
        closeImportModal();
    });
}

// ── Step 3: preview ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function loadPreview() {
    goStep(3);
    document.getElementById('previewLoading').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('previewContent').style.display = 'none';
    if (IS_EDITOR) document.getElementById('btn3Apply').disabled = true;

    fetch(URL_PREVIEW, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': CSRF },
        body: JSON.stringify({ datacenter: selectedDC, rule_texts: getRuleTexts() }),
    })
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => {
        document.getElementById('previewLoading').style.display = 'none';
        if (data.error) {
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').textContent = '❌ ' + data.error;
            return;
        }
        buildConfirmTable(data);
        document.getElementById('previewContent').style.display = 'block';

        const hasServers = data.total > 0;
        if (IS_EDITOR) {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btn3Apply');
            btn.disabled = !hasServers;
            btn.style.opacity = hasServers ? '1' : '.45';
            btn.style.cursor  = hasServers ? 'pointer' : 'not-allowed';
        }

        if (!hasServers) {
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').textContent =
                'ℹ No servers meet the criteria — all servers may have undetermined cluster or in_live_play values.';
        }
    })
    .catch(err => {
        document.getElementById('previewLoading').textContent = '❌ Network error: ' + err;
    });
}

// Store full server lists for modal access
let previewServersAll = {};

function buildConfirmTable(data) {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('confirmTbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    previewServersAll = {};

    data.summary.forEach((item, idx) => {
        const color = RULE_COLORS[item.rule_key] || '#aaa';

        // Store full server list for this rule
        previewServersAll[idx] = {
            servers:    item.servers,
            truncated:  item.truncated,
            count:      item.count,
            actionText: item.action_text,
            color:      color,
        };

        // Main row — chevron toggles chips, count link opens modal
        const tr = document.createElement('tr');
        tr.innerHTML = `
            <td>
                <button class="chips-toggle" data-idx="${idx}" title="Show/hide servers">
                    <span class="chevron">▶</span>
                </button>
                <span class="color-dot" style="background:${color}"></span>
                <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:bold;color:var(--text-muted);margin-right:6px;">${escHtml(RULE_LABELS[item.rule_key] || item.rule_key)}</span>
                <span style="font-size:12px;">${escHtml(item.action_text)}</span>
            </td>
            <td class="confirm-count">
                <button class="confirm-count-link" data-idx="${idx}">${item.count} server${item.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}</button>
            </td>
            <td></td>
        `;
        tbody.appendChild(tr);

        // Preview chips row (max 50) — hidden by default
        const preview   = item.servers.slice(0, 50);
        const remaining = item.count - preview.length;
        const trC = document.createElement('tr');
        trC.innerHTML = `<td colspan="3" style="padding-top:0;">
            <div class="server-chips" id="chips_${idx}">
                ${preview.map(s => `<span class="chip">${escHtml(s)}</span>`).join('')}
                ${remaining > 0
                    ? `<button class="confirm-count-link" data-idx="${idx}" style="font-size:10px;margin-left:4px;">
                           +${remaining} more…
                       </button>`
                    : ''}
            </div>
        </td>`;
        tbody.appendChild(trC);
    });

    const skippedNote = data.skipped > 0
        ? ` — ${data.skipped} skipped (undetermined values)`
        : '';
    document.getElementById('confirmTotal').textContent =
        `${data.total} servers${skippedNote}`;

    // Chevron toggle: show/hide chips
    tbody.querySelectorAll('.chips-toggle').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const idx    = btn.dataset.idx;
            const chips  = document.getElementById(`chips_${idx}`);
            const chev   = btn.querySelector('.chevron');
            const isOpen = chips.classList.toggle('open');
            chev.textContent  = isOpen ? '▼' : '▶';
            chev.style.color  = isOpen ? 'var(--btn-primary)' : '';
        });
    });

    // Count link and "+N more" — open detail modal
    tbody.querySelectorAll('.confirm-count-link').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => openSrvModal(parseInt(btn.dataset.idx)));
    });
}

// ── Server list modal ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function openSrvModal(idx) {
    const data  = previewServersAll[idx];
    if (!data) return;

    // Set title + dot color
    document.getElementById('srvModalDot').style.background   = data.color;
    document.getElementById('srvModalTitle').textContent      = data.actionText;
    document.getElementById('srvModalSearch').value           = '';
    document.getElementById('srvModalEmpty').style.display    = 'none';

    // Build list — server items use data-hostname for reliable filtering
    // Truncation note uses a separate class so it is never filtered
    const list  = document.getElementById('srvModalList');
    const total = data.servers.length;

    list.innerHTML = data.servers
        .map(s => `<div class="srv-modal-item" data-hostname="${escHtml(s)}">${escHtml(s)}</div>`)
        .join('');

    if (data.truncated) {
        list.innerHTML +=
            `<div class="srv-modal-trunc">… list capped at 200 — full count: ${data.count} servers.</div>`;
    }

    updateSrvModalCount(total, total);
    document.getElementById('srvModalOverlay').classList.add('open');
    // Reset search input and empty notice
    document.getElementById('srvModalSearch').value        = '';
    document.getElementById('srvModalEmpty').style.display = 'none';
    setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('srvModalSearch').focus(), 50);
}

function filterSrvModal(query) {
    const q     = query.toLowerCase().trim();
    const items = document.querySelectorAll('#srvModalList .srv-modal-item');
    let visible = 0;

    items.forEach(item => {
        const hostname = item.dataset.hostname.toLowerCase();
        const match    = !q || hostname.includes(q);
        item.classList.toggle('hidden', !match);
        if (match) visible++;
    });

    updateSrvModalCount(visible, items.length);
    document.getElementById('srvModalEmpty').style.display =
        (q && visible === 0) ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function updateSrvModalCount(visible, total) {
    const el = document.getElementById('srvModalCount');
    el.textContent = (visible === total)
        ? `${total} server${total !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`
        : `${visible} / ${total} servers`;
}

function closeSrvModal(event) {
    // Close only if clicking the overlay itself, not the modal
    if (event.target === document.getElementById('srvModalOverlay')) {
        document.getElementById('srvModalOverlay').classList.remove('open');
    }
}

// Close modal on Escape key
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        document.getElementById('srvModalOverlay').classList.remove('open');
    }
});

// ── Step 4: apply + polling ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
function startJob() {
    goStep(4);
    document.getElementById('progressWrap').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('successWrap').style.display  = 'none';
    document.getElementById('errorWrap').style.display    = 'none';
    document.getElementById('progressBar').style.width    = '0%';
    document.getElementById('progressStatus').textContent = 'Sending request...';
    document.getElementById('progressLog').innerHTML      = '';

    fetch(URL_START, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': CSRF },
        body: JSON.stringify({ datacenter: selectedDC, rule_texts: getRuleTexts() }),
    })
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => {
        if (data.error) { showError(data.error); return; }
        startPolling(data.job_id);
    })
    .catch(err => showError('Network error: ' + err));
}

function startPolling(jobId) {
    const url = URL_STATUS.replace('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', jobId);
    pollInterval = setInterval(() => {
        fetch(url)
            .then(r => r.json())
            .then(status => {
                if (status.error && !status.done) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval); showError(status.error); return;
                }
                updateProgress(status);
                if (status.done) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval);
                    status.error ? showError(status.error) : showSuccess(status);
                }
            })
            .catch(() => {}); // transient network error — retry next tick
    }, 800);
}

function updateProgress(status) {
    document.getElementById('progressBar').style.width    = (status.progress || 0) + '%';
    document.getElementById('progressStatus').textContent = status.message || '';
    const logEl = document.getElementById('progressLog');
    (status.log || []).forEach(entry => {
        const id = `log_${entry.ts}_${entry.msg.slice(0,20)}`;
        if (!document.getElementById(id)) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.id = id;
            const ok = entry.msg.startsWith('✓');
            div.innerHTML = `<span class="log-ts">${entry.ts}</span>`
                          + `<span class="${ok ? 'log-ok' : 'log-inf'}">${escHtml(entry.msg)}</span>`;
            logEl.appendChild(div);
            logEl.scrollTop = logEl.scrollHeight;
        }
    });
}

function showSuccess(status) {
    document.getElementById('progressWrap').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('successWrap').style.display  = 'block';
    const s = status.stats || {};
    document.getElementById('successMsg').textContent =
        `${s.total || '?'} servers updated in ${s.datacenter || selectedDC}.`;
}

function showError(msg) {
    document.getElementById('progressWrap').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('errorWrap').style.display    = 'block';
    document.getElementById('errorMsg').textContent       = '❌ ' + msg;
}

// ── Reset ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function resetWizard() {
    if (pollInterval) clearInterval(pollInterval);
    selectedDC = null;
    initTexts();
    currentStep = 1;
    document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('visible'));
    document.getElementById('step1').classList.add('visible');
    updateStepper();
    // Reload DC cards — loadDatacenters() will pre-select the last used DC
    loadDatacenters();
}

// ── Util ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function escHtml(str) {
    return String(str)
        .replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
}

// ── Init ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
loadDatacenters();
initTexts();

window.goStep         = goStep;
window.loadPreview    = loadPreview;
window.startJob       = startJob;
window.resetWizard    = resetWizard;
window.restoreDefault = restoreDefault;
window.closeSrvModal  = closeSrvModal;
window.filterSrvModal = filterSrvModal;
window.handleImportFile        = handleImportFile;
window.closeImportModal        = closeImportModal;
window.closeImportModalOverlay = closeImportModalOverlay;
window.confirmImport           = confirmImport;

// ── Live SVG sync ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Maps each rule input to its corresponding SVG text cell (data-cell-id)
const SVG_CELL_MAP = {
    cluster_yes_lp_yes: 'rB_text',
    cluster_yes_lp_no:  'rC_text',
    no_yes_phys:        'rD_text',
    no_yes_virt_fixed:  'rE_text',
    no_yes_virt_str:    'rF_text',
    no_no_phys:         'rG_text',
    no_no_virt_fixed:   'rH_text',
    no_no_virt_str:     'rI_text',
    hypervisor:         'rJ_text',
};

function updateSvgCell(ruleKey, text) {
    const cellId = SVG_CELL_MAP[ruleKey];
    if (!cellId) {
        return;
    }

    const cellGroup = document.querySelector(`[data-cell-id="${cellId}"]`);
    if (!cellGroup) {
        return;
    }

    const displayText = (text || '').trim() || ' ';

    // Try foreignObject first (present only if cell had text at export time)
    const fo = cellGroup.querySelector('foreignObject');
    if (fo) {
        const selectors = [
            'foreignObject div div div',
            'foreignObject div div',
            'foreignObject div',
        ];
        let updated = false;
        for (const sel of selectors) {
            const el = cellGroup.querySelector(sel);
            if (el) {
                el.textContent = displayText;
                updated = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!updated) {
        }
    } else {
        // No foreignObject — cell was empty at export time.
        // Fallback: use the SVG <text> element, or create a foreignObject.
    }

    // Fallback: SVG <text> element
    const textEl = cellGroup.querySelector('text');
    if (textEl) {
        textEl.textContent = displayText.length > 35
            ? displayText.slice(0, 33) + '…'
            : displayText;
    }

    // Last resort: cell has no foreignObject and no <text>.
    // This happens when the draw.io cell was exported empty.
    // We create a foreignObject dynamically so the text is visible.
    if (!fo && !textEl) {
        const svgNS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
        const htmlNS = 'http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml';

        // Read the cell group's bounding rect to size the foreignObject
        const rect = cellGroup.querySelector('rect');
        const x  = rect ? parseFloat(rect.getAttribute('x')  || 0) : 0;
        const y  = rect ? parseFloat(rect.getAttribute('y')  || 0) : 0;
        const w  = rect ? parseFloat(rect.getAttribute('width')  || 160) : 160;
        const h  = rect ? parseFloat(rect.getAttribute('height') || 54)  : 54;

        const newFo = document.createElementNS(svgNS, 'foreignObject');
        newFo.setAttribute('x', x);
        newFo.setAttribute('y', y);
        newFo.setAttribute('width',  w);
        newFo.setAttribute('height', h);
        newFo.setAttribute('requiredFeatures',
            'http://www.w3.org/TR/SVG11/feature#Extensibility');

        const outerDiv = document.createElementNS(htmlNS, 'div');
        outerDiv.setAttribute('xmlns', htmlNS);
        outerDiv.style.cssText =
            'display:flex;align-items:center;' +
            `width:${w}px;height:${h}px;overflow:hidden;`;

        const innerDiv = document.createElementNS(htmlNS, 'div');
        innerDiv.style.cssText =
            'font-size:10px;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;' +
            'text-align:left;word-break:break-word;padding:2px 6px;';
        innerDiv.textContent = displayText;

        outerDiv.appendChild(innerDiv);
        newFo.appendChild(outerDiv);
        cellGroup.appendChild(newFo);
    }
}

function initSvgSync() {

    let bound = 0;
    ALL_RULES.forEach(key => {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`inp_${key}`);
        if (!inp) {
            return;
        }

        // Live update on every keystroke
        inp.addEventListener('input', () => updateSvgCell(key, inp.value));
        bound++;

        // Initial sync with current textarea value
        updateSvgCell(key, inp.value);
    });
}

// Load SVG from static file, inject inline, then sync texts
function loadDiagramSvg() {
    const wrap = document.getElementById('bcDiagramSvg');

    // Avoid re-fetching if SVG is already loaded
    if (wrap.querySelector('svg')) {
        initSvgSync();
        return;
    }

    const url = "{% static 'businesscontinuity/img/bc_diagram.svg' %}";
    fetch(url)
        .then(r => {
            if (!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status);
            return r.text();
        })
        .then(svgText => {
            wrap.innerHTML = svgText;
            // The SVG's own light-dark() colors (edges, text) follow the OS/browser
            // color-scheme preference, not this app's independent dark-mode toggle.
            // Force it explicitly so it always matches what the user actually sees.
            const svgEl = wrap.querySelector('svg');
            if (svgEl) {
                const appIsDark = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark-mode';
                svgEl.style.colorScheme = appIsDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
            }
            // Wait two animation frames so foreignObject divs are in the DOM
            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                    initSvgSync();
                });
            });
        })
        .catch(() => {
            wrap.innerHTML =
                '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);padding:20px 0;">Diagram unavailable.</p>';
        });
}

// SVG is loaded via loadDiagramSvg() called from goStep(2)

})();
</script>

</body>
</html>

    <script>

        const isDarkMode = localStorage.getItem('darkMode') === 'true';
        if (isDarkMode) {
            document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark-mode');
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            const form = document.getElementById('csv-upload-form');
            const uploadButton = document.getElementById('submitButton');
            const messagesContainer = document.getElementById('messages');
            const detailedErrorsContainer = document.getElementById('detailed-errors');
            const fileInput = form.querySelector('input[type="file"]');
            const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
            const progressBar = document.getElementById('progressBar');
            const progressMessage = document.getElementById('progressMessage');
            const formatBadge = document.getElementById('formatBadge');
            const importTypeInput = document.getElementById('importType');

            const FORMATS = {
                server: {
                    key: 'server',
                    label: 'Server Import',
                    requiredHeaders: ['hostname_unique'],
                    badgeClass: 'server',
                },
                application: {
                    key: 'application',
                    label: 'Application Import',
                    requiredHeaders: ['application_id', 'in_live_play'],
                    badgeClass: 'application',
                },
            };

            let detectedFormat = null;

            function detectFormat(headers) {
                if (headers.includes('hostname_unique')) return FORMATS.server;
                if (headers.includes('application_id')) return FORMATS.application;
                return null;
            }

            function updateButtonState(isDisabled) {
                uploadButton.disabled = isDisabled;
                if (isDisabled) {
                    uploadButton.classList.add('disabled');
                } else {
                    uploadButton.classList.remove('disabled');
                }
            }

            updateButtonState(true);

            fileInput.addEventListener('change', function () {
                formatBadge.style.display = 'none';
                formatBadge.className = '';
                detectedFormat = null;
                importTypeInput.value = '';

                if (fileInput.files.length === 0) {
                    updateButtonState(true);
                    return;
                }

                const file = fileInput.files[0];
                const previewReader = new FileReader();
                previewReader.onload = function (event) {
                    const firstLine = event.target.result.split('\n')[0] || '';
                    const headers = firstLine.trim().split(';').map(h => h.trim());
                    detectedFormat = detectFormat(headers);

                    formatBadge.style.display = 'inline-block';
                    if (detectedFormat) {
                        formatBadge.textContent = 'Detected format: ' + detectedFormat.label;
                        formatBadge.className = detectedFormat.badgeClass;
                        importTypeInput.value = detectedFormat.key;
                        updateButtonState(false);
                    } else {
                        formatBadge.textContent = 'Unknown format — check the header row';
                        formatBadge.className = 'unknown';
                        updateButtonState(true);
                    }
                };
                previewReader.readAsText(file);
            });

            form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();

                updateButtonState(true);
                messagesContainer.innerHTML = '';
                detailedErrorsContainer.style.display = 'none';
                detailedErrorsContainer.innerHTML = '';
                progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
                progressBar.style.width = '0%';
                progressMessage.textContent = 'Starting import...';

                const file = fileInput.files[0];
                if (!file) {
                    alert("Please select a CSV file.");
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    updateButtonState(false);
                    return;
                }

                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function (event) {
                    const contents = event.target.result;
                    const lines = contents.split('\n').filter(line => line.trim() !== '');

                    if (lines.length < 2) {
                        alert("This CSV file has no data!");
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        updateButtonState(false);
                        return;
                    }

                    const headers = lines[0].trim().split(';').map(h => h.trim());
                    const format = detectFormat(headers);

                    if (!format) {
                        alert("Unknown CSV format. Please check the header row against the supported formats.");
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        updateButtonState(false);
                        return;
                    }

                    const missingHeaders = format.requiredHeaders.filter(h => !headers.includes(h));
                    if (missingHeaders.length > 0) {
                        alert("Missing required columns: " + missingHeaders.join(', '));
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        updateButtonState(false);
                        return;
                    }

                    const dataLines = lines.slice(1);
                    if (!confirm(`${format.label}: import ${dataLines.length} records?`)) {
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        updateButtonState(false);
                        return;
                    }

                    const formData    = new FormData(form);
                    let   pollInterval = null;

                    // Step 1 — POST to start the background import job
                    fetch(form.action, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' },
                        body: formData,
                    })
                    .then(r => r.json())
                    .then(data => {
                        // Handle synchronous validation errors returned before the job starts
                        if (data.error) {
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            handleSSEData(data);
                            updateButtonState(false);
                            return;
                        }
                        if (!data.job_id) {
                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                            const li = document.createElement('li');
                            li.className = 'error';
                            li.textContent = 'Could not start import job.';
                            messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                            updateButtonState(false);
                            return;
                        }

                        // Step 2 — poll for progress every 800ms
                        const statusUrl = `{% url appname|add:':import_csv_status' job_id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' %}`.replace('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', data.job_id);

                        pollInterval = setInterval(() => {
                            fetch(statusUrl)
                                .then(r => r.json())
                                .then(status => {
                                    progressBar.style.width     = `${status.progress || 0}%`;
                                    progressMessage.textContent = status.message || '';

                                    if (status.done) {
                                        clearInterval(pollInterval);
                                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';

                                        if (status.error) {
                                            handleSSEData({ error: status.error });
                                        } else if (status.stats) {
                                            // Re-use existing handleSSEData with the stats payload
                                            handleSSEData({ success: status.stats.message });
                                            if (status.stats.has_warnings && status.stats.errors) {
                                                handleSSEData({
                                                    warning:        `${status.stats.errors.length} warnings detected.`,
                                                    detailed_errors: status.stats.errors,
                                                });
                                            }
                                        }

                                        updateButtonState(true);
                                        fileInput.value           = '';
                                        formatBadge.style.display = 'none';
                                        formatBadge.className     = '';
                                        importTypeInput.value     = '';
                                        detectedFormat            = null;
                                    }
                                })
                                .catch(() => {}); // transient network error — retry next tick
                        }, 800);
                    })
                    .catch((error) => {
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        const li = document.createElement('li');
                        li.className = 'error';
                        li.textContent = `An error occurred: ${error}`;
                        messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                        updateButtonState(false);
                    });
                };
                reader.readAsText(file);
            });

            function handleSSEData(data) {
                if (data.progress !== undefined) {
                    progressBar.style.width = `${data.progress}%`;
                    progressMessage.textContent = `${data.message || 'Processing...'} ${data.progress.toFixed(0)}%`;
                }
                if (data.warning) {
                    const li = document.createElement('li');
                    li.className = 'warning';
                    li.textContent = data.warning;
                    messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                }
                if (data.error) {
                    const li = document.createElement('li');
                    li.className = 'error';
                    li.textContent = data.error;
                    messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                }
                if (data.success) {
                    const li = document.createElement('li');
                    li.className = 'success';
                    li.textContent = data.success;
                    messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                }
                if (data.detailed_errors) {
                    detailedErrorsContainer.style.display = 'block';
                    let ul = document.createElement('ul');
                    data.detailed_errors.forEach(err => {
                        let li = document.createElement('li');
                        li.textContent = err;
                        ul.appendChild(li);
                    });
                    detailedErrorsContainer.appendChild(ul);
                }
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>
